A: I have 10 hotels that allow pets. One is Aloft Atlanta Downtown, which is rated 4.0.

A: Their number is +1 404-877-9000.

A: Would you like to reserve rooms in the hotel?
U: No, not right now.
A: Anything else I can help you with?

U: Yeah, that's great. Can you give me the venue address?
A: The reservation has been confirmed, hope you enjoy the show! The venue is located at 1280 Peachtree Street Northeast.
U: Thanks so much for all your help.
A: Can I do anything else for you?
U: No thanks, you've done everything I needed.
A: Have a nice day.
